    SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[ApprovedAt], [c].[ApprovedBy], [c].[CaseType], [c].[ClosedAt], [c].[ClosedBy], [c].[CollectedAmount], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[CreatedBy], [c].[Description], [c].[ExtraDetails], [c].[RejectedAt], [c].[RejectedBy], [c].[RejectionReason], [c].[Status], [c].[TargetAmount], [c].[Title], [c].[Location_Latitude], [c].[Location_Longitude]
    FROM [Cases] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[Status] = @statuses1
    ORDER BY [c].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [c1]
LEFT JOIN [CaseAttachment] AS [c0] ON [c1].[Id] = [c0].[CaseId]

SELECT [c1].[Id], [c1].[ApprovedAt], [c1].[ApprovedBy], [c1].[CaseType], [c1].[ClosedAt], [c1].[ClosedBy], [c1].[CollectedAmount], [c1].[CreatedAt], [c1].[CreatedBy], [c1].[Description], [c1].[ExtraDetails], [c1].[RejectedAt], [c1].[RejectedBy], [c1].[RejectionReason], [c1].[Status], [c1].[TargetAmount], [c1].[Title], [c1].[Location_Latitude], [c1].[Location_Longitude], [c0].[Id], [c0].[CaseId], [c0].[FileName], [c0].[FilePath], [c0].[FileType], [c0].[UploadDate]
FROM (
    SELECT [c].[Id], [c].[ApprovedAt], [c].[ApprovedBy], [c].[CaseType], [c].[ClosedAt], [c].[ClosedBy], [c].[CollectedAmount], [c].[CreatedAt], [c].[CreatedBy], [c].[Description], [c].[ExtraDetails], [c].[RejectedAt], [c].[RejectedBy], [c].[RejectionReason], [c].[Status], [c].[TargetAmount], [c].[Title], [c].[Location_Latitude], [c].[Location_Longitude]
    FROM [Cases] AS [c]
    WHERE [c].[Status] = @statuses1
    ORDER BY [c].[CreatedAt] DESC
    OFFSET @p ROWS FETCH NEXT @p2 ROWS ONLY
) AS [c1]
LEFT JOIN [CaseAttachment] AS [c0] ON [c1].[Id] = [c0].[CaseId]
ORDER BY [c1].[CreatedAt] DESC, [c1].[Id]
